# KTM 150 SX 2021: aftermarket parts

4830 listings whose stated fitment names this bike: 397 researched parts (with what they change and rider findings), 742 dealer and retailer listings specific to this bike, and 3691 universal retailer listings (oil, grips, batteries and the like, counted below; every listing is in [parts.csv](../parts.csv)). Research candidates: check fitment on the maker's page before relying on a row. Back to [the family catalog](../CATALOG.md).
